00207: def spow(v,e):
00208:     return math.copysign(abs(v)**e,v)
00209: 
00210: def cushion(name,center,scale,material,ex=.34,ez=.55,rotation=(0,0,0)):
00211:     verts=[];faces=[]
00212:     nu,nv=64,28
00213:     for j in range(nv+1):
00214:         p=-math.pi/2+math.pi*j/nv
00215:         for i in range(nu):
00216:             t=2*math.pi*i/nu
00217:             verts.append((scale[0]*spow(math.cos(p),ez)*spow(math.cos(t),ex),
00218:                           scale[1]*spow(math.cos(p),ez)*spow(math.sin(t),ex),
00219:                           scale[2]*spow(math.sin(p),ez)))
00220:     for j in range(nv):
00221:         for i in range(nu):
00222:             a=j*nu+i;b=j*nu+(i+1)%nu
00223:             faces.append((a,b,b+nu,a+nu))
00224:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00225:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces);mesh.update()
00226:     obj=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh);bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00227:     obj.location=center;obj.rotation_euler=rotation;obj.data.materials.append(material)
00228:     for p in mesh.polygons:p.use_smooth=True
00229:     furniture_extra.append(obj)

00214:             t=2*math.pi*i/nu
00215:             verts.append((scale[0]*spow(math.cos(p),ez)*spow(math.cos(t),ex),
00216:                           scale[1]*spow(math.cos(p),ez)*spow(math.sin(t),ex),
00217:                           scale[2]*spow(math.sin(p),ez)))
00218:     for j in range(nv):
00219:         for i in range(nu):
00220:             a=j*nu+i;b=j*nu+(i+1)%nu
00221:             faces.append((a,b,b+nu,a+nu))
00222:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name);mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces);mesh.update()
00223:     obj=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh);bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00224:     obj.location=center;obj.rotation_euler=rotation;obj.data.materials.append(material)
00225:     for p in mesh.polygons:p.use_smooth=True
00226:     return obj
